a:T751,The authors build a fully verifiable full-plan benchmark on 4x4 sliding puzzles, including blocked-cell variants. The model sees an initial board and a goal board, then must output the entire action sequence at once. The evaluator replays the sequence step by step and stops at the first illegal move; there is no skipping, correction, or partial credit for reaching the goal midway through a longer invalid sequence.

To make efficiency verifiable, the benchmark uses a restricted family of boards with certified optimal lengths. For a state s, h(s) is defined as the sum of shortest free-cell graph distances from each tile to its goal cell. Since each move can reduce this quantity by at most one, h(s) is a lower bound on the optimal solution length. The dataset retains only boards for which a witness solution of length exactly h(s) exists, so the optimum d* is certified as d*=h(s). This avoids trusting the model and avoids expensive online exact search for every evaluation case.

The main metric is success-weighted plan efficiency, SPE = R × Q × E. R is execution reliability: the fraction of outputs that are fully legal. Q is planning coordination: among legal plans, the fraction that actually reaches the goal. E is action economy: among completed plans, the average ratio d*/L between optimal length and produced length. This decomposition is useful because it cleanly separates three failure modes: illegal execution, legal but incomplete planning, and completed but inefficient planning. It also yields SPE = C × E, where C is completion rate, so failed attempts still affect the overall score instead of disappearing from efficiency analysis.

The primary cohort controls both exact optimal horizon d* in {10, 20, 30, 40} and obstacle count k in {0, 1, 2, 3}, with 20 boards per cell. A separate stress cohort uses longer horizons with more obstacles.b:T554,The study evaluates 8,400 complete-plan outputs from seven model conditions. Each condition has 960 outputs on the primary cohort and 240 on the stress cohort, with three trials per board. In the primary cohort, Astra, Fable 5, and Opus 5.5 all achieve R=1 and Q=1, meaning every output is legal and reaches the goal. But their action economy differs materially: E is about 0.830, 0.807, and 0.745, corresponding to average excess lengths of 8.33, 9.65, and 13.01 moves over the certified optimum. This is the central empirical point: identical success can conceal meaningful differences in planning efficiency.

Other models fail in different ways. Some lose mostly on legality, some on legal-but-incomplete long-horizon coordination, and some complete reliably but with much longer plans. The paper reports bootstrap confidence intervals and resamples at the board level rather than the individual-output level, preserving the balanced design.

An important caveat is that the certified-optimum construction restricts the benchmark family. Because the retained boards satisfy d*=h(s), they emphasize long-sequence coordination without requiring heuristic retreat or temporary detours. So the benchmark is clean and verifiable, but it does not cover the full space of harder planning instances where optimal solutions must move away from local heuristic progress.c:T8f4,The model uses a shared 24-layer bidirectional Transformer backbone with model dimension 512. Inputs are converted into token-level embeddings, processed jointly, mean-pooled, projected to a 768-dimensional embedding, and L2-normalized. Text uses a standard embedding table. Images, video, and audio are each mapped by modality-specific encoders into soft token embeddings that enter the same shared backbone. This is the key difference from classic dual-tower retrieval models: mixed-modal inputs can interact inside the sequence model before pooling.

A central architectural change is the simplified per-layer embedding design. Gemma-style PLE normally uses an additional token lookup table to inject input-conditioned residual information into each layer. Here, the model reuses the main input embeddings and modality encoder outputs instead of maintaining a separate PLE lookup table. Each layer applies a layer-specific projection, normalization, and gating mechanism to inject this information. The ablations indicate that this simplification is not merely cheaper; it also works better for this embedding setting.

Training follows a three-stage curriculum. The early stage emphasizes text-only learning to stabilize language and code representations. Later stages introduce multimodal tasks and broader domains. Distillation from Gemini Embedding 2 is separated from contrastive and regularization objectives: the model first inherits a strong global embedding geometry from the teacher, then undergoes contrastive fine-tuning to adapt the space to its own architecture and retrieval behavior. This separation is important. Pure distillation can preserve teacher structure, but it may overconstrain the student space; contrastive adaptation gives the student a chance to optimize for its own embedding geometry.

The deployment story is also part of the method. The shared backbone is only 271M parameters; adding vision yields 439M, adding audio yields 577M, and the full model is 744M. The system supports selective encoder loading, adjustable visual token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantization-aware checkpoints. So the design target is not just benchmark quality, but a modular multimodal embedding stack that can be served under different resource budgets.d:T757,The full model has 744M parameters: a 271M shared backbone, a 167M vision encoder, and a 305M audio encoder. The backbone has 24 layers and outputs 768-dimensional embeddings. Evaluation spans seven broad benchmark suites covering text, multilingual text, code, image, video, documents, speech, and environmental sound tasks. The headline claim is that EmbeddingGemma 2 defines a new quality-efficiency Pareto frontier across modalities, often outperforming specialist systems and models that are 2x to 12x larger.

The most useful evidence comes from the ablations. First, simplified PLE outperforms the version with a dedicated lookup table while reducing cost, suggesting that preserving an input-to-layer semantic pathway matters more than maintaining a separate PLE vocabulary. Second, reducing the visual token budget has limited impact on many tasks, but hurts document retrieval much more, which is consistent with the need for fine-grained spatial and layout information in document understanding. Third, co-training the vision encoder is important; weak adaptation or freezing degrades the shared embedding space. Fourth, contrastive fine-tuning after distillation is better than relying on distillation alone. Fifth, native audio understanding beats an ASR-then-text pipeline, indicating that non-lexical acoustic information contributes materially to embedding quality.

The paper summary does not expose every benchmark number in the provided excerpt, but the full-text claims are specific enough: despite using fewer text parameters than its predecessor and adding more modalities, the model improves on multilingual text and code benchmarks, while often matching or exceeding much larger multimodal systems on multimodal evaluations. For a researcher, the main value is not the leaderboard itself, but which training and architectural choices produced those gains.e:T4ca,The paper operationalizes the “view from nowhere” principle via Population-Centered Evolution (PCE).

PCE has two stages:
1) Population distillation: for a chosen task, a distillation agent scans task records and modification histories across all evaluated variants and produces a structured population block with:
- Outcome evidence: frequency of outcomes across the population (is a failure systemic or isolated?).
- Observation: a cross-agent comparative hypothesis explaining why some variants succeed and others fail.
- Candidate files: pointers to harness components likely responsible, narrowing the edit search space.
2) Diagnosis and harness modification: the system checks the population-level hypothesis against the selected parent’s traces and source code, then proposes a concrete harness change.

A key design choice is separating the evidence set (all evaluated variants 𝒱) from the parent-eligible archive (𝒜 ⊆ 𝒱). Even variants that will never be selected as parents can still contribute through distilled evidence. Compared with GEA/DGM/HGM, the novelty is less about selection heuristics and more about turning population diversity into explicit diagnostic input for targeted consolidation.f:T460,Dwell is a model-based RL framework that reframes a pretrained VLM as a recurrent latent transition operator in a POMDP. From histories of observations and actions, it infers a belief state b_t in the VLM’s latent space, predicts transitions and rewards in that space, and uses imagined latent rollouts to improve the policy.

Two implementation choices carry most of the novelty:
- Latent imagination over reconstruction: following joint-embedding style representation learning, it avoids synthesizing observations at every step and instead models predictable structure in a compact latent, reducing imagination cost.
- Dual adapters on one backbone: world/reward modeling and policy learning are instantiated as separate LoRA adapters attached to the same VLM backbone. This keeps a shared representation interface (mitigating latent incompatibility) while enabling parameter-efficient, partially decoupled online updates.

The trade-off is explicit: give up pixel-level generative fidelity to gain cheaper rollouts and a tighter coupling between the latent used for prediction and the latent consumed by the policy.10:T51f,WorkFactory is a pipeline that makes task environments verifiably executable before collecting teacher trajectories.

1) Professional intent library: intents are derived from occupational hierarchies, skill documentation, and real deliverables, so the target output and audience are defined before instantiating materials.

2) Information Support Graph (backward planning): starting from the intended deliverable, the system plans dependencies at the level of information items (premises/conditions) and specifies where each supporting fact should appear across reused/synthesized artifacts, while explicitly leaving derived quantities for the solver.

3) Probe execution + execution-based repair: after materialization, an isolated probe attempts the task using only solver-visible inputs to detect support failures (missing/contradictory premises, broken dependencies). Repairs must restore support without changing the intended work or leaking key derivations.

4) Independent teacher collection: only tasks that pass probe+repair are assigned to a separate teacher agent to generate trajectories for SFT.

5) Component analysis: planning and repair are treated as separable modules; the paper argues they are complementary—planning reduces structural defects, repair catches instantiation-induced defects.11:T4d1,Task Advancement Memory (TAM) uses verified task progress as both online state and offline supervision.

1) Instruction compilation into a requirement graph 𝒬: an LLM-based compiler decomposes the instruction into requirements (info to acquire or outcomes to achieve), dependency edges, per-requirement evidence rules for verification from GUI observations, and optional branch conditions.

2) Task Advancement State (online): maintains each requirement’s status (unresolved/established), known values, evidence, and object bindings. When an observation satisfies an evidence rule, TAM records an advancement and updates the remaining-goal representation—what is done, what inputs are available, and which goals are ready next.

3) Advancement supervision (retrospective): each advancement marks candidate interaction segments; after the episode, TAM selects/abstracts segments that caused verified local results into persistent operations specified by required inputs at entry, the result to achieve, and how to verify it. This salvages useful skills from both successful and failed trajectories.

4) Plug-in design: TAM augments a fixed base policy by providing bounded memory context; it does not retrain the policy itself.3:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L9",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-10-29","generated_at":"2026-10-30T15:42:52.975227+00:00","stats":{"total":202,"starred":3,"must_read":11,"skim":88,"glance":103,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":8,"synthetic_data":1,"llm_pretrain":5,"scaling":3,"architecture":8,"efficient":20,"eval":35,"multimodal":9,"multimodal_gen":13,"post_train":29,"mechanism":32,"coding_agent":7,"agent":32},"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"ok"}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以真实预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。合成/自举数据归 synthetic_data；训练 recipe/优化器归 llm_pretrain；评测资产归 eval","papers":[{"id":"2610.36983","title":"Advancement or Acceleration? The main questions are accuracy across hallucination types, whether the reported probabilities are usable, and whether decision-model ensembles can improve reliability.","importance":"The contribution is mainly about evaluation infrastructure rather than pretraining algorithms. Hallucination judges are often used in model evaluation, data filtering, RAG validation, and post-training data construction. If the judge is expensive or poorly calibrated, it becomes a noisy training signal. The useful conclusion is conditional: decision models can be competitive for some hallucination types, but they need type-specific validation before being used as drop-in replacements.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose hallucination detection into five binary tasks: factual contradiction, factual fabrication, context inconsistency, logical inconsistency, and instruction inconsistency. They build a 9,852-item benchmark from six existing datasets and preserve the original evaluation units and class distributions. Each model receives the same response and instruction, plus source context when the task is context inconsistency. Decision models return probabilities over H and N. The generative baseline, gpt-6-sol, is prompted to return a structured verdict and probability estimates so that all judges can be compared under the same protocol. The important design choice is to analyze judge behavior by error type and by probability quality, not just by aggregate accuracy.","experiments":"The study evaluates five decision models—jev, d1, clef, pplx, and gpt-6-luna—against gpt-6-sol. Performance varies substantially by hallucination type. The best decision model matches gpt-6-sol in balanced accuracy on context inconsistency, is within about 3 percentage points on factual errors, and trails by roughly 4–8 points on logical and instruction inconsistency. The probability outputs are mostly mildly overconfident rather than unusable, which makes them helpful for routing. Confidence-based cascades further improve detection, suggesting that decision models are useful components in low-cost evaluation pipelines even when they are not uniformly strong standalone judges.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Read the task taxonomy and dataset mapping first, because the binary label construction determines what the results mean. Then focus on the per-type performance tables and the calibration analysis. The confidence-cascade section is the most practically relevant part for large-scale evaluation systems. evolution. Evaluation has two roles: it provides feedback that steers the search, and it decides which scaffold is finally returned. These roles have different statistical and operational requirements, so treating all evaluation as generic fitness estimation wastes budget.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model pretraining is indirect but useful for automated optimization loops. Modern LLM systems increasingly optimize scaffolds, tool-use programs, data-generation workflows, and post-training pipelines. In these settings, evaluation cost can dominate the search process. The paper’s main value is the separation between cheap comparable feedback during search and more reliable discrimination at final selection. The same pattern can inform checkpoint selection, data-mixture search, SFT/RL data filtering, and other expensive evaluation loops.","technical_approach":"The authors formalize a scaffold-item execution as an evaluation cell. Full evaluation runs every candidate scaffold on the full task set, which is costly because each item may involve multiple LLM calls, retrieval operations, and tool interactions. EvalAlloc splits the process into search-time evaluation and final selection. During search, a short fully evaluated warm-up identifies a shared informative core and calibrates a response model. Later candidates are evaluated on sparse item subsets centered around that core, while item parameters are kept fixed to maintain a common scoring reference across iterations. After search, the method uses warm-started paired successive halving, reusing observations already collected and only running missing evaluations where needed. A refitted response model supports comparisons in later selection rounds.","experiments":"The evaluation uses four benchmarks and two scaffold-evolution frameworks, with three independent runs per setting. EvalAlloc uses roughly half as many evaluation cells as full evaluation while achieving competitive or higher mean final performance in the reported settings. The ablations indicate that informative item allocation and the final-selection stage are the main contributors. Replay analyses show why: shared informative items help compare closely matched candidates, and a common scoring reference preserves comparability across evolutionary iterations. The search gains are concentrated in early iterations, while the final choice remains sensitive to uncertainty among top candidates.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Trajectory replay and certifi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-28","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_36415.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"arxiv_url":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36415","source_url":null,"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的问题很窄，但切得很干净：如果不允许工具调用、外部搜索、执行反馈和中途修复，LLM 自身一次性生成完整计划的“内生规划能力”到底到什么程度。作者认为，很多 planning 评测把模型能力和外部 verifier/search 混在一起，成功率高并不等于模型真的会规划；而只看成功率或只看成功样本的路径长度，也看不出失败发生在合法性、完成度还是效率上。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值有限，它不是基座训练方法论文，也不讨论数据、优化或建模范式。但它对 reasoning/planning eval 有一个可取之处：把 planning 结果拆成可验证的三个因子，避免只看 pass rate 得出过粗的结论。若你关心 post-train 后模型的规划边界、或者想找一种更细粒度的 reasoning 评测协议，这篇可以参考；若只关心 pretrain recipe，本篇优先级不高。","technical_approach":"作者构造了一个严格可验证的 sliding puzzle 全计划评测。任务要求模型看到初始盘面和目标盘面后，一次性输出完整动作序列，不能边执行边修正。评测器逐步 replay 动作，遇到第一个非法动作就停止，不做跳过或修复。为了让“效率”可验证，作者只保留一类带证书的 puzzle：对状态 s，定义 h(s) 为每个 tile 到目标位置的最短自由格图距离之和。由于每一步最多让一个 tile 的该距离减少 1，h(s) 是下界；再配合一个长度正好为 h(s) 的 witness 解，就能证明最优步数 d*=h(s)。这样就不需要信任模型，也不需要对每个样本在线跑昂贵搜索。\n\n核心指标是 SPE = R × Q × E。R 是 execution reliability，表示整条计划完全合法的比例；Q 是 planning coordination，表示在合法计划中真正到达目标的比例；E 是 action economy，表示成功计划的最优步数与实际步数之比 d*/L。这个分解有两个好处。第一，它把非法执行、合法但未完成、完成但冗长三类失败分开。第二，SPE=C×E，其中 C 是完成率，因此失败样本通过 C 进入总分，而不是像只统计成功样本长度那样被忽略。实验设计上，主 cohort 控制 4×4 puzzle 的精确最优步长 d*∈{10,20,30,40} 和障碍数 k∈{0,1,2,3}，每个格子 20 个盘面；另有更长步长的 stress cohort。","experiments":"总共评测 8,400 个完整计划输出，来自 7 个模型条件，每个条件在主 cohort 上 960 个输出，在 stress cohort 上 240 个输出，每个盘面做 3 次试验。主结果里，Astra、Fable 5、Opus 5.5 在 primary cohort 上达到 R=1、Q=1，也就是所有输出都合法且完成目标，但三者 E 不同，分别约为 0.830、0.807、0.745，对应平均比最优多 8.33、9.65、13.01 步。这说明只看成功率会把“都会做”和“做得是否经济”混为一谈。\n\n其余模型在 R、Q、E 上出现不同短板。作者强调，有些模型主要败在执行合法性，有些主要败在合法但无法完成长程协调，还有些即便都能完成，路径也明显更长。论文还给出 bootstrap 置信区间，并按 board 而不是单次输出重采样，保持平衡设计。另一个值得注意的限制是：为了让 d*=h(s) 可证，样本族被限制为一种每步都可让启发式下降的 puzzle，因此它测的是长序列协调，不覆盖必须暂时“绕路”或 heuristic retreat 的更一般规划难题。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看方法定义和结果表，不必陷入 puzzle 细节。重点是理解为什么作者要把 planning 分成 R、Q、E 三层，以及为什么 SPE 比单独 success rate 或 optimality gap 更稳。然后看数据构造部分里 d*=h(s) 的可验证证书，这决定了整个评测的可信度，也同时决定了它的适用边界。最后看结果表里那些成功率相同但 E 不同的模型对比，这正是论文最想说明的点。预计 15 到 25 分钟足够抓住主要内容。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but well-posed question: how much planning ability do LLMs exhibit on their own when they must produce a complete plan in a single response, with no tools, no execution feedback, no search, and no repair? Many planning evaluations confound model capability with external verification or search. A high success rate under tool use does not isolate innate planning, and reporting only success or only the path length of successful cases hides where failure actually occurs.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is limited. new","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-28","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2610_37364.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CV","cs.SD"],"arxiv_url":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37364","source_url":null,"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个很具体但长期悬而未决的问题：能否用不到 1B 参数的统一 embedding 模型，原生处理 text、image、audio、video 以及它们的交错混合输入，同时在检索、匹配、跨模态对齐和混合模态理解上维持接近或超过专用大模型的质量。已有路线大致有两类：一类是 CLIP/SigLIP 这类双塔模型，跨模态检索强，但不能自然编码 interleaved mixed-modal input；另一类是从 MLLM 改造成 embedding 模型，能力更全，但参数和推理成本很高。EmbeddingGemma 2 的核心问题就是在统一表示、混合模态建模和部署效率之间找一个更实用的平衡点。","importance":"对基座研究的意义不在于提出新的生成式预训练范式，而在于把“统一表征模型”这条线做得更工程化、更可复用。它给出几个有参考价值的结论。第一，统一 embedding 不一定要依赖超大 MLLM，744M 量级也能把多模态表示做到较强水平。第二，训练上把 text-only 与 multimodal 分阶段处理、把 distillation 与 contrastive finetuning 分开，确实比一锅炖更稳。第三，架构上简化 per-layer embeddings，去掉专门的 PLE lookup table，不只是省参数，质量还更好。第四，音频模态的原生建模优于先做 ASR 再走文本 embedding，这对“把新模态直接接入统一 LM/encoder”有直接启发。若关心统一 tokenizer/统一表示、轻量级多模态预训练、以及 embedding serving，这篇有实际参考价值。","technical_approach":"模型由三部分组成。共享主干是一个 24 层、d_model=512 的双向 Transformer，最终做 mean pooling，再经线性投影到 768 维并做 L2 normalization，得到检索用单位向量。文本直接走 token embedding；图像、视频、音频分别由独立模态编码器转成 soft token embeddings，再送入同一个双向主干，因此 mixed-modal 输入可以在 token 级交互，而不是像双塔那样只在向量空间对齐。\n\n一个关键设计是对 Gemma 系列里的 per-layer embeddings 做了简化。原始 PLE 需要额外的大型 token lookup table，给每层提供输入侧残差信号。这里改成直接复用主 embedding table 和模态编码器输出的输入表示，再通过 layer-specific projection、RMSNorm 和 gate 注入各层。这样做减少了专门参数，也避免了额外词表带来的冗余。论文的消融显示，这个简化版 PLE 比带独立 lookup table 的版本更好。\n\n训练是三阶段 curriculum。第一阶段偏 text-only，先把语言和代码 embedding 基础打稳；第二阶段再引入 multimodal 任务和模态扩展；第三阶段把蒸馏目标与对比学习/正则化目标拆开处理，先从更强教师模型 Gemini Embedding 2 学全局空间结构，再用 contrastive finetuning 把学生自己的 embedding geometry 调整到更适合检索。这里的判断很清楚：蒸馏擅长快速继承教师分布，但如果一直只蒸馏，学生空间往往受教师约束过强；后续单独做 contrastive 调整，能更贴近目标任务。\n\n另一个值得注意的点是模块化部署。共享 backbone 只有 271M；加视觉编码器是 439M；加音频编码器是 577M；全模态 744M。也就是说它不是一个必须整包加载的多模态模型，而是按模态裁剪。再加上可调视觉 token budget、可截断 embedding 维度、以及量化感知训练版本，目标很明确：把统一多模态 embedding 做成可部署系统，而不是只做 benchmark 模型。","experiments":"模型总参数 744M，其中 backbone 271M、vision encoder 167M、audio encoder 305M；主干 24 层，输出 embedding 维度 768。论文评测覆盖七类综合 benchmark，涉及 text、multilingual text、code、image、video、document、speech、sound 等任务。作者的主结论是：在跨全部模态的综合评测上，EmbeddingGemma 2 形成了新的质量-成本 Pareto 前沿，常常超过专用模型以及 2 到 12 倍更大的模型。\n\n从全文给出的实验信息看，几个消融最有信息量。第一，简化 PLE：去掉专门 lookup table 后，成本下降且效果提升，说明对 embedding 模型来说，层间输入注入更重要的是“保留输入语义通路”，不一定需要额外词表参数。第二，vision token budget：训练和推理时减少视觉 token，对大多数视觉任务影响有限，但 document retrieval 这类依赖细粒度版面和局部视觉细节的任务下降明显，这说明视觉 token 压缩的代价主要出现在高分辨率结构理解，而不是一般图文语义对齐。第三，vision encoder 共训很重要；如果只冻结或弱适配视觉塔，统一空间质量会受损。第四，后期 contrastive finetuning 优于只做 distillation。第五，原生音频理解优于 ASR 转文本方案，说明环境声、韵律、非语言声学信息确实能转化为 embedding 质量收益。\n\n摘要里没有列出完整逐项数字榜单，但正文的主张比较明确：即便文本参数比前代更少，同时新增 audio/video 等模态，文本和代码 benchmark 仍有提升；多模态任务上则经常达到或超过多十亿参数模型的水平。对读者来说，更重要的是这些提升来自哪些 recipe，而不只是最终分数。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三件事。第一，看架构部分里 simplified PLE 的公式和对应消融表，重点理解为什么复用输入 embedding 比单独 lookup table 更合适。第二，看训练部分的三阶段 curriculum，尤其是 text-only 与 multimodal 分离、distillation 与 contrastive 分离的安排，这部分对做统一 encoder 训练很有借鉴意义。第三，看消融实验里 vision token budget、vision encoder co-training、audio native vs ASR 的对比，这几项直接对应实际系统设计。\n\n如果时间有限，建议阅读顺序是：Introduction → 模型结构图与 PLE 描述 → 训练阶段说明 → 主要 benchmark 总表 → 消融表。预计 25 到 40 分钟可以抓住主要信息。若你关心多模态预训练接口而不是 embedding 产品本身，重点看 mixed-modal tokenization 进入共享 backbone 的方式，以及训练目标如何从教师蒸馏过渡到学生自己的对比空间。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical but nontrivial question: can a sub-1B multimodal embedding model natively encode text, images, audio, video, and arbitrary interleaved mixtures into one shared space, while remaining strong on retrieval-style tasks across modalities and still being cheap enough for deployment? Existing options are unsatisfying in opposite ways. Dual-encoder families such as CLIP or SigLIP are efficient and strong for cross-modal matching, but they do not naturally model mixed-modal inputs. MLLM-derived embedding models can handle interleaved multimodal context, but they are usually several billions of parameters and expensive to serve. EmbeddingGemma 2 is aimed at this gap.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is less about a new generative pretraining objective and more about a credible recipe for unified multimodal representation learning under realistic compute constraints. Several takeaways matter. First, unified multimodal embeddings do not strictly require a very large MLLM backbone; a 744M model can be competitive across text, code, image, video, and audio. Second, separating text-only and multimodal training as curriculum, and separating distillation from contrastive adaptation, appears to be a meaningful training choice rather than a cosmetic one. Third, simplifying per-layer embeddings by removing the dedicated PLE lookup table improves both efficiency and quality. Fourth, native audio modeling beats the ASR-to-text fallback, which is relevant to any effort that wants to treat new modalities as first-class inputs rather than textual proxies.","technical_approach":"$c","experiments":"$d","reading_guide":"For a skim-level read, focus on three parts. 有相似结构：不是只看最优样本，而是利用分布统计与对照来定位可迁移的规律。更现实的价值在于：在固定 foundation model 参数、只改 harness 的设定下，PCE 用更少 token 达到更高成功率，说明群体证据可以显著降低试错成本；这对大模型推理成本敏感的 agent 迭代很关键。","technical_approach":"方法核心是“view from nowhere”原则的工程化：改进决策不应只基于 parent-local 证据，而要基于全体 population 的成功/失败与自修改历史的模式。\n\nPCE（Population-Centered Evolution）分两阶段：\n1) Population distillation：对选定任务，收集所有已评估变体的 task records（执行记录、结果）与 modification histories（做过哪些 harness 改动），由一个 distillation agent 生成 population block。population block 由三类字段组成：\n- Outcome evidence：群体里不同结果出现的频率，用来判断某个失败是偶发还是系统性。\n- Observation：跨 agent 的对照观察，尝试解释“为什么有的变体成功、有的失败”。这一步本质是从对照样本中抽取可操作的诊断假设。\n- Candidate files：把假设落到可修改的 harness 位置（哪些文件/模块可能相关），降低后续修改的搜索空间。\n2) Diagnosis & modification：对选中的 parent，先用 population block 提供的假设去核对 parent 的执行记录与源码，再提出具体 harness 修改。\n\n与 GEA/DGM/HGM 的差异点在于证据组织方式：GEA 更像在小群体内共享经验；PCE 把“群体作为对照集”显式结构化成可消费的诊断输入，并且把“证据用到谁身上”和“谁有资格当 parent”解耦（维护全体变体集合 𝒱 与可选 parent 的 archive 𝒜）。这使得大量不再被选为 parent skills）、真实职业交付物（professional deliverables）。意图先定义“要产出什么交付物/面向谁/质量标准”，再去实例化材料，避免先拼材料再硬凑任务导致目标漂移。\n\n(2) Information Support Graph（信息支持图，信息级规划）\n不是把“文件 A 链到文件 B”，而是显式表示：目标输出依赖哪些信息项/前提条件/中间判断，这些信息应该出现在哪些可见工件中；同时刻意把应由 solver 推导的中间量留空（例如成本比较中不直接给出生命周期成本）。这一步相当于对任务做 backward chaining：从最终 deliverable 反推所需 premises，并把 premises 的“落点”绑定到环境材料。\n\n(3) Probe execution + execution-based repair（探测执行与基于执行的修复）\n材料实例化（检索/复用/合成文档、表格等）后，容易引入缺失/冲突。WorkFactory 用一个隔离的 probe（只看 solver 可见输入）尝试执行任务，定位 support failure（缺信息、矛盾、不可达的依赖等）。修复的约束是“恢复支持但不改变意图与工作量边界”：补齐前提、消除矛盾，但不把关键推导结果直接塞给 teacher。\n\n(4) Teacher trajectory collection（独立 teacher 收集示范）\n只有通过 probe+repair 的环境才交给独立 teacher 执行，产出轨迹用于 SFT。这里的设计意图是把“环境缺陷”与“teacher 能力不足”尽量解耦，提升示范一致性。\n\n(5) 数据 scaling 与组件贡献分析\n论文还把信息级规划与执行修复作为可拆组件，做缺陷率与下游性能的对照，主张两者互补：规划减少结构性缺陷，修复捕捉实例化带来的偶发缺陷。","experiments":"数据与训练：构造 112,686 个任务，覆盖 612 change","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-28","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_37482.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"arxiv_url":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482","source_url":null,"summary":{"core_problem":"GUI 用可验证的中间进展（task advancement）定义状态与监督，能把长轨迹切成更干净的 credit assignment 单元；\n- 把可复用操作表示成“输入条件—目标结果—验证规则”，比直接记动作序列更抗界面变化，类似把交互数据做成程序/技能库的中间表示。\n整体属于 skim：方法清晰，但与 tokenizer/scaling/优化等预训练核心议题距离较远。","technical_approach":"Task Advancement Memory (TAM) 把“任务推进”作为在线状态与离线监督的共同锚点。\n\n(1) Task compiler → requirement graph 𝒬\n初始化时用 LLM 编译指令，把任务拆成若干 requirements（需要获取的信息或需要达成的结果），并用依赖边表示输入供给关系；每个 requirement 还带 evidence rule（如何从 GUI 观察验证已满足）与可选的分支条件（何时需要该 requirement）。这一步把任务从“动作序列”改写成“结果依赖图”。\n\n(2) Task Advancement State（在线）\n维护每个 requirement 的状态（未解决/已建立）、已知值、证据与 object bindings（把值绑定到任务实体）。当 GUI 观察满足某个 evidence rule，就产生一次 advancement，并据此更新“剩余任务”：哪些目标已满足、哪些输入已就绪、下一步哪些目标可推进。它解决的是跨应用继续执行时的“我已经做到了哪、还缺什么”。\n\n(3) Advancement supervision（离线/事后）\n每次 advancement 会标记一段候选交互片段；任务结束后回看 episode record，从成功与失败轨迹中抽取真正导致 verified local result 的片段，并抽象成可复用 operation：进入所需输入、要达成的结果、以及验证方式。这样把“失败任务里的有效部分”也变成能力资产。\n\n(4) 与 base policy 的关系\nTAM 不重训 base policy，而是作为外部记忆/状态管理模块，向固定策略提供 bounded memory context；强调方法是 memory/state 层面的改造，而不是端到端策略学习。","experiments":"评测基准：MobileWorld 的 GUI-only 子集、SPA-Bench 的 Level-3 English
